'bg-[#880E4F] text-white shadow-md' : 'text-gray-400 hover:bg-rose-50'}`}>{s}</button>m                 ))}m               </div>m            </div>m          </aside>mm          {/* Nội dung chính */}m          <div className="flex-1 space-y-12">m            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-rose-50 pb-6">m               <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold font-playfair text-[#880E4F]">m                 {activeTab === 'docs' ? 'Thư viện Tài liệu' : 'Phòng Luyện Đề'} Lớp {selectedGrade} - {selectedSubject}m               </h2>m               <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-300 uppercase italic">🌸 Chất lượng • Uy tín • Dịu dàng</div>m            </div>mm            {activeTab === 'docs' ? (m              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">m                {filteredDocs.map(doc => (m    <div key={doc.id} className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-rose-50 overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-xl transition-all flex flex-col group">m                    <div className="h-40 bg-rose-50 flex items-center justify-centerrelative overflow-hidden">m                       <FileText size={48} className="text-rose-200 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform"/>m                       <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 bg-white/80 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-[8px] font-bold text-rose-400">{doc.format}</div>m                    </div>m                    <div className="p-6 space-y-4 flex-1 flex flex-col">m                      <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm line-clamp-2 h-10">{doc.title}</h4>m                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[9px] font-bold text-gray-300 uppercase">m                        <span>{doc.pages} Trang</span>m                        <span className="text-rose-400">{doc.price.toLocaleString()}đ</span>m                      </div>m                      <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">m                         <button onClick={() => setViewItem({ ...doc, type: 'preview' })} className="flex-1 bg-rose-50 text-rose-500 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[9px] font-bold uppercase flex items-center justify-center gap-1"><Eye size={12}/> Xem thử</button>m                         <button onClick={() => setViewItem({ ...doc, type: 'buy' })} className="flex-1 bg-rose-500 text-white py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[9px] font-bold uppercase shadow-md">Mua ngay</button>m           </div>m                    </div>m                  </div>m                ))}m              </div>m   ) : (m              <div className="space-y-6">m                {filteredExams.map(exam => (m                  <div key={exam.id} className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl border border-rose-50 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between group hover:border-rose-200 transition-all">m                    <div className="flex items-center gap-6">m    <div className={`w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-white ${exam.type === 'trắc nghiệm' ? 'bg-amber-400' : 'bg-rose-400'}`}>m                        {exam.type === 'trắc nghiệm' ? <ListChecks size={24}/> : <PenTool size={24}/>}m                      </div>m                      <div>m                        <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{exam.title}</h4>m                        <p className="text-[9px] font-bold text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">{exam.questions} Câu • {exam.time} Phút</p>m                      </div>m  </div>m                    <div className="flex gap-3">m                      {exam.canTry && (m  <button onClick={() => setActiveExam({ ...exam, isTrial: true })} className="bg-rose-50 text-rose-500 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[9px] font-bold uppercase border border-rose-100">Thi thử</button>m                      )}m      <button onClick={() => checkUnlocked(exam.code) ? setActiveExam(exam) : showToast("Bạn cần mua mã để thi chính thức!", "info")} className="bg-[#880E4F] text-white px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[9px] font-bold uppercase shadow-sm">Bắt đầu</button>m                    </div>m                  </div>m                ))}m              </div>m            )}m         </div>m        </div>m      )}mm      {/* Khu vực Làm bài */}m      {activeExam && (m        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-white animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-5">m           <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto py-12 px-6 space-y-8 h-full flex flex-col">m              <div className="flex justify-between items-center">m                 <button onClick={() => setActiveExam(null)} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-rose-500 font-boldtext-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest"><ArrowLeft size={16}/> Quay lại</button>m                 <div className="px-5 py-2 bg-rose-500 text-white rounded-full font-bold text-xs flex items-center gap-2">m                   <Clock size={14}/> {activeExam.time}:00 {activeExam.isTrial && "(Bản thử)"}m                 </div>m              </div>m              <div className="bg-rose-50/50 p-8 rounded-[2.5rem] border border-rose-100 flex-1 flex flex-col">m                 <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold font-playfair text-center mb-10 text-[#880E4F]">{activeExam.title}</h2>m                 m            {activeExam.type === 'tự luận' ? (m                   <div className="space-y-6 flex-1 flex flex-col">m                 <div className="p-6 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-rose-100 text-sm italic text-gray-500">m                {activeExam.isTrial ? "[ĐỀ THỬ]: Anh/chị hãy viết đoạn văn 200 chữ về ý nghĩa của sự sẻ chia." : "Đề bài chính thức đang đợi bạn chinh phục..."}m                      </div>m                      <textarea m       value={essayAnswer} onChange={e => setEssayAnswer(e.target.value)}m                        placeholder="Cầm bút lên và viết nên những trang chữ của riêng bạn..."m                        className="w-full flex-1 p-8 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-rose-100 outline-none focus:ring-2 ring-rose-200 resize-none text-sm leading-loose"m   />m                      <button onClick={handleGradeEssay} disabled={loading} className="w-full bg-[#880E4F] text-white py-5 rounded-2xl font-bold text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest shadow-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2">m                        {loading ? <Sparkles size={16} className="animate-spin"/> : <Brain size={16}/>} {loading ? 'Đang chấm...' : 'Nộp bài & Chấm AI'}m                      </button>m                   </div>m                 ) : (m              <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400 italic text-sm">Nội dung đề trắc nghiệm đang tải...</div>m                 )}m              </div>m           </div>m        </div>m      )}mm      {/* Modal Xem thử / Mua */}m      {viewItem && (m        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-rose-950/20 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">m           <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-lg rounded-[2.5rem] shadow-2xl p-10 space-y-8 animate-in zoom-in-95">m              <div className="flex justify-between items-start">m                 <div className="flex items-center gap-3">m                    <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-rose-50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-rose-500"><FileText size={20}/></div>m                    <div>m                       <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{viewItem.title}</h3>m                       <p className="text-[9px] font-bold text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-widest">{viewItem.category} Lớp {viewItem.grade}</p>m                    </div>m            </div>m                 <button onClick={() => setViewItem(null)} className="text-gray-300 hover:text-rose-500"><X size={20}/></button>m              </div>mm              {viewItem.type === 'preview' ? (m                <div className="space-y-4">m                   <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-rose-300 uppercase tracking-widest">Nội dung xem trước (10%):</p>m                   <div className="p-6 bg-rose-50/30 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-rose-200 text-xs text-gray-500 leading-relaxed italic">m                      {viewItem.preview}m                      <div className="mt-4 text-center text-rose-300">[Phần nội dung còn lại đã bị ẩn]</div>m                   </div>m           <button onClick={() => setViewItem({...viewItem, type: 'buy'})} className="w-full bg-rose-500 text-white py-4 rounded-2xl font-bold text-[10px] uppercase shadow-lg">Mua để xem toàn bộ</button>m                </div>m ) : (m                <div className="space-y-6">m                   <div className="p-6 bg-amber-50 rounded-2xl border border-amber-100">m                      <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-700 mb-2">Thanh toán chuyển khoản</h4>m                      <p className="text-[10px] text-amber-600 leading-relaxed italic">Vui lòng chuyển <b>{viewItem.price.toLocaleString()}đ</b> tới STK 12345678 - MB Bank (Nội dung: {viewItem.code}). Mã mở khóa sẽ được gửi vào email của bạn sau 1-5 phút.</p>m                   </div>m                   <button onClick={() => { setViewItem(null); showToast("Đã gửi yêu cầu thanh toán!", "info"); }} className="w-full bg-[#880E4F] text-white py-4 rounded-2xl font-bold text-[10px] uppercase shadow-lg">Tôi đã thanh toán</button>m                </div>m              )}m           </div>m </div>m      )}mm      {/* Auth Modal */}m      {authMode && (m         <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-rose-950/20 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">m           <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="bg-white w-full max-w-xs rounded-[2.5rem] shadow-2xl p-10 space-y-6 animate-in zoom-in-95 relative">m              <button type="button" onClick={() => setAuthMode(null)} className="absolute top-6 right-6 text-gray-300"><X size={18}/></button>m           <div className="text-center space-y-2">m                 <h3 className="text-xl font-bold font-playfair text-[#880E4F]">Ghé Tiệm</h3>m                 <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 italic">Dành cho những tâm hồn mộng mơ</p>m              </div>m              <input name="email" type="email" required placeholder="Email của bạn..." className="w-full px-5 py-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 text-xs outline-none focus:ring-1 ring-rose-200 font-medium" />m <input name="password" type="password" required placeholder="Mật khẩu..." className="w-full px-5 py-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 text-xs outline-none focus:ring-1 ring-rose-200 font-medium" />m              <button className="w-full bg-[#880E4F] text-white py-3.5 rounded-xl font-bold text-[10px] uppercase shadow-lg">Xác nhận</button>m           </form>m</div>m      )}mm      {/* Toast */}m      {toast && (m        <div className={`fixed bottom-10 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 px-8 py-4 rounded-full text-white font-bold text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest z-[200] animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-5 shadow-2xl ${toast.type === 'error' ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-[#AD1457]'}`}>m          {toast.msg}m        </div>m )}mm      <footer className="py-20 bg-white border-t border-rose-50 text-center space-y-6">m         <div className="flex justify-center gap-2">m            <div className="w-6 h-6 bg-rose-100 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-rose-500"><Feather size={12}/></div>m            <span className="text-xs font-bold font-playfair text-[#880E4F]">Tiệm Chữ 𐙚</span>m         </div>m         <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-300 font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.5em]">Lưu giữ những trang chữ dịu dàng nhất</p>m      </footer>mm      <style>{`m        @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Playfair+Display:ital,wght@0,400;0,700;1,400;1,700&family=Quicksand:wght@300;400;500;600;700&display=swap');m        body { font-family: 'Quicksand', sans-serif; scroll-behavior: smooth; }m        .font-playfair { font-family: 'Playfair Display', serif; }m        ::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 4px; }m        ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: #FFE4E9; border-radius: 10px; }m      `}</style>m    </div>m  );m};mmexport default App;
